Wn=w[n-1-m] (negative lag -m)

 # pair i, i+m
 # plus term h_{i+m}(1-h_i): ∂h_i = -Wp h_{i+m}; ∂h_{i+m}= Wp (1-h_i)

Plus lag0.

`fft_corr_lags(h, n, nfft=None)`:
  b = 1.0 - h ; b_rev = b[::-1]
  N = 2*n-1; if nfft is None: nfft=1 << (N-1).bit_length()

Now, implementing L-BFGS with FFT gradient. Need test mentally with small n for projection of gradient. Let's outline functions:

```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0, 1)
    target = 0.5*n
    # iterative largest room adjustment
    hsum = h.sum()
    if hsum == target: return h
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff = target - hsum
       if abs(diff) <= 1e-14: break
       if diff > 0:
          room = 1.0 - h
          tot = room.sum()
          if tot <= 1e-15: break
          # add proportional to room, but cap to avoid overshooting? diff/tot*room <= room if diff<=tot? target<=n, room sum = n-sum. If diff positive and sum<target<=n, diff <= n-sum? target - sum <= n - sum yes. So ok.
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot = h.sum()
          if tot <=1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
       hsum = h.sum()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This preserves sum approximate. To make exact, final adjust:
```
def fix_sum_exact(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2.0

  val=target-s
  if 0<=val<=1: h[-1]=val
  else:
    # distribute to all interior indices
    # if too high, reduce from non-zero; low add to non-full
    ...
  return h

Optimization using L-BFGS-B:
```
def lbfgsb_step(h,n,alpha,pen,deadline,maxiter=200, ftol=1e-18, gtol=1e-12, maxls=40):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    nfft=_get_nfft(n)
    _LBF_N=n; _LBF_NFFT=nfft; _LBF_ALPHA=alpha; _LBF_PEN=pen; _LBF_TARGET=n/2.0; _LBF_DX=2.0/n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    while True:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       try:
          res=minimize(_lbf_objective_grad, h, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol,'gtol':gtol,'maxls':maxls,'iprint':-1})
          h = project_feasible(np.clip(res.x,0,1),n)
          c=compute_c5(h,n)
          if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
       except Exception:
          break
       # if res.status in (1,4?) and no improvement maybe break? But could continue with same h? L-BFGS-B may converged; break if status=0 and c not improved? To avoid endless.
       if res.status == 0 and c >= best_c - 1e-15:
          break
       if time.time() > deadline: break
    return best_h, best_c
```
But if `res.status` is 2 (maxls) and no improvement, maybe break. We'll just loop with maxiter chunk but if no improvement and status not line search error? Use `iters_left`? Simpler: call once per phase with maxiter estimate, not loop. But to respect deadline, chunk loop with e.g., 100 iters. If converged, break. If no improvement after a chunk, maybe break to save time? Could still improve with slightly different? We'll break if c not improved and res.status in (0,2) and maxiter>...? But maybe nonlinear. Add `no_improve_count`.

However each `minimize` call allocates memory h full; okay.

Need set `maxiter` based on alpha/n. Maybe use `chunk = min(300, max(20, int(remaining / 2.0)))`? But we don't know time per iter. We can set fixed chunks: for n<=600 chunk=200; n<=1000 chunk=150; else 80. The while loop can run multiple chunks. If no improvement in two chunks, break.

def lbfgsb_with_limit(h, n, alpha, deadline, pen=1e4, min_iters=20, max_chunk=2000):
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5

      # estimate

      maxit=min(maxit,max_chunk)
      t0=time.time()
      try: res=minimize(... maxiter=maxit ...)
      except: break
      h=project_feasible(np.clip(res.x,0,1),n)
      c=compute_c5(h,n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-15:
         best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c; no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve += 1
      if no_improve >= 3: break

Local search top-level functions:
Need top-level `add_lag_gradient(grad, lag, h, n, weight)`.
```
def lag_gradient_inplace(grad, lag, h, n, weight):
  m=abs(lag)
  if m==0:
    grad += weight*(1-2*h)
  elif lag>0:
    # c = sum h[i+m]*(1-h[i])
    grad[:n-m] += -weight*h[m:]
    grad[m:] += weight*(1-h[:n-m])
  else:
    grad[:n-m] += weight*(1-h[m:])
    grad[m:] += -weight*h[:n-m]
```
This is raw gradient. Add weight.

Local active search:
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng,use_fft=False, K=20, beta=100? ):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
  iters=0; no_improve=0
  while time<deadline:
    c=compute_all_lags_scaled(h,n,fft=(n>1200))
    C=c.max()
    if C < best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
    else: no_improve+=1 maybe
    # weights = softmax(beta*(c-C)) but beta maybe 50? active near max
    z=beta*(c-C); z_z=z - z.max(); w=np.exp(z_z); w/=w.sum()
    # top indices where w>...
    active = np.argsort(w)[::-1][:K]
    # direction 1: weighted gradient
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active:
       if w[idx] < 1e-8: continue
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       lag_gradient_inplace(g, lag, h, n, w[idx])
    g -= g.mean()
    norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
    improved=False
    if norm>1e-12:
       # line search with multiple steps
       scale = 1.0/(norm*0.05?)?
       # Try steps: 0.5/norm? Since g raw gradient; objective change ~ step*||g||^2? Use `steps = [1.0/(1+norm), 0.5/..., 0.1/...]` times 0.1
       for f in [0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,1e-4]:
           cand = h - f*g
           cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
           ct=compute_c5(cand,n)
           if ct < best_c - 1e-15:
              h=cand; best... improved=True; no_improve=0; break
           # undo
       if not improved:
          # try negative of top single lag maybe? But g includes.
          # transfer directions:
          for idx in active[:min(5,K)]:
             lag=idx-(n-1); if lag==0 continue
             d=construct_transfer_dir(h,lag,n,weight=1)
             d -= d.mean(); normd=...
             for f in [0.05,0.02,0.005,0.001]:
                cand=h+f*d; project; if improve accept
             if improved break
    if not improved: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > some: break or random restart
       # random small perturbation
       h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0, 0.002, n), n)
       if compute_c5(h)>best? keep h? maybe not harm.
    iters+=1
  return best_h,best_c
```

Transfer direction construction:
```
def transfer_direction(h, lag, n, weight=1.0):
  d=np.zeros(n); m=abs(lag)
  if lag>0:
    diff = h[m:] - h[:n-m]
    # for all i where later>earlier, move from later to earlier: earlier +, later -
    d[:n-m] += diff
    d[m:] -= diff
  else:
    diff = h[:n-m] - h[m:]
    d[:n-m] -= diff
    d[m:] += diff
  return weight*d
```
This is sum over all pairs; entries can accumulate O(m) up to n. Use scale by max abs to control.

Maybe use only positive components of diff to avoid moving where would increase other? For positive lag, if diff positive, moving helps; if diff negative, d moves opposite which may increase current lag. But aggregated direction with all diff is gradient-like? The exact maximizing of c_m? To reduce c_pos, we want h_i and h_{i+m} more similar. The unconstrained least squares to make h_i=h_{i+m} would adjust by half difference; direction diff positive: earlier up, later down; diff negative: earlier down, later up. So diff all signs is good to reduce spread, not just term c_pos? If h_i<h_j (diff positive), c_pos term h_j(1-h_i) positive; moving from j to i reduces. If h_i>h_j, c_pos term smaller but h_i(1-h_j) (negative lag) might be large; moving from i to j reduces negative. So all diff reduces both squared disagreement. Good.

But objective max over both signs; combining all active lags with diff aggregated is like reducing variance along those lags. Good.

Could use this direction directly; likely fast.

We define c_lags from np.correlate: c_idx = ∑_{i} h_{i+ℓ}(1-h_i) for ℓ>=0; c_-ℓ = ∑ h_i(1-h_{i+ℓ}). Pair contributions as above.

c0 = ∑ h_i(1-h_i); raw gradient (1-2h_i)*w0.
For m>0:
Positive c_m = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-m} h_{i+m}(1-h_i). Grad:
∂h_i (i=0..n-m-1): -h_{i+m}
∂h_j (j=m..n-1): 1-h_{j-m}
Negative c_-m = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-m} h_i(1-h_{i+m}). Grad:
∂h_i: 1-h_{i+m}
∂h_{i+m}: -h_i
Combine with weights Wp, Wn:
For i< n-m:
 grad_i += (-Wp - Wn) h_{i+m} + Wn*1
For j=i+m:
 grad_j += (Wp - Wn) h_j? Wait formula: grad_j += Wp(1-h_i) - Wn h_i = Wp - (Wp+Wn)h_i. Good.
For i>=? symmetric.
Our conv formula:
grad_i = w0(1-2h_i) + const_left(i)+const_right(i) - ∑_{m=1}^{i}q_m h_{i-m} - ∑_{m=1}^{n-1-i}q_m h_{i+m}
where q_m=Wp+Wn, const_left=∑_{m=1}^{i}Wp, const_right=∑_{m=1}^{n-1-i}Wn.
For a coordinate i:
 contributions from pairs to right m=1..n-1-i: const includes Wn_m, h term -q_m h_{i+m}=-(Wp+Wn)h_{i+m}. Sum = Wn - (Wp+Wn)h_{i+m} -> matches for left endpoint of pair.

`smooth_value_weights(c, alpha)`:
```
m = np.max(c)
z = alpha*(c - m)
mx = np.max(z)
ez = np.exp(z - mx)
s = ez.sum()
if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
   # fallback uniform on max indices
   w = np.zeros_like(c); maxidx = (c == m); w[maxidx]=1; w/=w.sum()
   return float(m), w
w = ez/s
val = float(m + (mx + np.log(s))/alpha)
return val,w
```
If alpha very large and z-mx negative huge, exp underflows to 0 for many; at least max ez=1. Good.

`lbf_objective_grad` uses `val, w = smooth...`. If val not finite, return large.

`lbfgsb_with_limit`:
Need set globals before each minimize. But objective may be called after globals change? Single-thread. Good.
```
def lbfgsb_with_limit(h, n, alpha, deadline, pen=1e4, min_chunk=10, max_chunk=1000):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    nfft = next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    _OBJ_N=n; _OBJ_NFFT=nfft; _OBJ_ALPHA=float(alpha); _OBJ_PEN=float(pen); _OBJ_TARGET=n/2.0; _OBJ_DX=2.0/n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 2: break
       # time objective
       t0=time.time()
       try: val,grad=lbf_objective_grad(h)
       except Exception: break
       t_eval=max(1e-7, time.time()-t0)
       # max iterations expected: rem/(t_eval*line_search_factor) ; line search factor maybe 8
       maxit = int(rem / (max(1e-7, 8.0*t_eval)))
       if maxit < min_chunk: maxit = min_chunk
       if maxit > max_chunk: maxit = max_chunk
       if maxit < 2: break
       t0=time.time()
       try:
          res = minimize(lbf_objective_grad, h, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                         options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol':1e-18, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':40, 'iprint':-1})
       except Exception:
          break
       h_new = np.clip(res.x,0,1)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)):
          break
       h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
       c = compute_c5(h_new, n)
       if c < best_c - 1e-15:
           best_c = c; best_h = h_new.copy(); h = h_new; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           # if no improvement and iteration time large, maybe still acceptable; break if 4
       if no_improve >= 4:
           break
       if res.status == 0 and no_improve > 0:
           # converged without improving; break
           break
       if time.time() >= deadline:
           break
    return best_h, best_c
```
Maybe `res.status==0` and no improvement but smooth optimum lower than exact? If computed c not improve, likely no further. Break.
Potential wasted time: If high alpha and L-BFGS-B status 0 but c improved tiny? no_improve resets if improved >1e-15. Good.

But if res.status==0 and c improved exactly by 1e-16, no improve; break. Fine.

Now, local search implementation more carefully:

```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng, max_dir=15, beta=50, use_fft=False):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
  no_improve=0
  it=0
  while time.time() < deadline:
    c = compute_all_lags_scaled(h,n, fft=use_fft)
    C = float(np.max(c))
    if C < best_c - 1e-15:
       best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
    # if best_c <= 0.3808? maybe continue? no, keep improving.
    z = beta*(c - C)
    # z.max=0; if beta too high, active one. Use beta=20 to include near max.
    ez=np.exp(z); # z<=0 so exp<=1 safe
    w=ez/ez.sum()
    top = np.argsort(w)[::-1][:max_dir]
    improved=False
    # Direction 1: weighted gradient
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in top:
       wi = w[idx]
       if wi <= 1e-8: continue
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       add_lag_gradient(g, lag, h, n, wi)
    g -= g.mean()
    if np.max(np.abs(g)) > 1e-14:
       s0 = 0.04 / (np.max(np.abs(g)) + 1e-14)
       for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
          cand = h + s0*f*g  # note g is gradient, so ascend? We need negative gradient: g defined as gradient of active c. To decrease, direction = -g. Use -g.
          cand = project_feasible(cand,n)
          ct=compute_c5(cand,n) or fast
          if ct < best_c - 1e-15:
              best_c=ct; best_h=cand.copy(); h=cand; improved=True; break
    if improved: no_improve=0; it=0; continue
    # Direction 2: transfer diff for top lags
    for idx in top[:5]:
       lag=idx-(n-1)
       if lag==0: continue
       d=transfer_direction(h,lag,n,1.0)
       d -= d.mean()
       if np.max(np.abs(d)) <= 1e-14: continue
       s0=0.04/(np.max(np.abs(d))+1e-14)
       for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
          cand=h+s0*f*d
          cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
          ct=compute_c5(cand,n)
          if ct < best_c - 1e-15:
             best_c=ct; best_h=cand.copy(); h=cand; improved=True; break
       if improved: break
    # Direction 3: binaryization
    if not improved:
       bdir = np.where(h >= 0.5, 1.0 - h, h)
       bdir -= bdir.mean()
       if np.max(np.abs(bdir))>1e-14:
          s0=0.03/(np.max(np.abs(bdir))+1e-14)
          for f in [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
             cand=h+s0*f*bdir
             if improve
    if improved:
       no_improve=0
    else:
       no_improve += 1
       it += 1
       if no_improve >= 30:
          # random restart from best
          h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0, 0.005, n), n)
          no_improve = 0
          if it % 100 ==0 and time.time()<deadline-2:
             # small random perturbation maybe larger 0.02 every x
    if no_improve > 200: break
  return best_h, float(best_c)
```
Need ensure we don't set h to perturbed if worse? We set perturb only for exploration, but best remains. If perturbed value much worse, next iterations try to recover. Good.

Now, `upsample_constant`:
```
def upsample_constant(h_src,n_src,n_dst):
  if n_dst == n_src: return project_feasible(h_src,n_dst)
  # old centers at (i+0.5)/n_src of domain [0,1] (length normalized)
  # new centers (j+0.5)/n_dst
  idx = np.floor(((np.arange(n_dst)+0.5) * (n_src / n_dst))).astype(np.int64)
  idx = np.clip(idx,0,n_src-1)
  h_dst = h_src[idx]
  return project_feasible(h_dst,n_dst)
```
If n_dst not multiple, some cells unequal. Good.
Downsample if needed:
```
def downsample_constant(h_src,n_src,n_dst):
  idx = np.floor(((np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(n_src/n_dst)))...
  # average over old cells in each new cell? simple mean of selected? If n_dst smaller, flip mapping old to new:
  h_dst=np.zeros(n_dst)
  counts=zeros
  old_to_new = np.clip(np.floor((np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(n_dst/n_src)),0,n_dst-1)
  np.add.at(h_dst, old_to_new, h_src)
  counts = np.bincount(old_to_new,n,n_dst)
  h_dst[counts>0]/=counts; return project.
```

`lbf_objective_grad` detailed:
```
def lbf_objective_grad(x):
    n=_OBJ_N; nfft=_OBJ_NFFT; dx=_OBJ_DX
    h=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)
    b=1.0-h; b_rev=b[::-1]
    B=np.fft.rfft(b_rev,nfft)
    c_raw=np.fft.irfft(H*B,nfft)[:2*n-1]
    c_scaled=c_raw*dx
    val,w=smooth_max_value_weights(c_scaled,_OBJ_ALPHA)
    # active threshold
    active = np.where(w > 1e-11)[0]
    if active.size < max(64, n//8):
        grad_raw=np.zeros(n)
        for idx in active:
            weight=w[idx]
            lag=idx-(n-1)
            if lag==0: grad_raw += weight*(1.0-2.0*h)
            elif lag>0:
                grad_raw[:n-lag] += -weight*h[lag:]
                grad_raw[lag:] += weight*(1.0-h[:n-lag])
            else:
                m=-lag
                grad_raw[:n-m] += weight*(1.0-h[m:])
                grad_raw[m:] += -weight*h[:n-m]
        grad=grad_raw*dx
    else:
        # FFT formula
        wp=w[n:n]
        wn=w[n-2::-1]
        q=wp+wn
        bL=np.zeros(n); bL[1:]=q
        bR=np.zeros(n); bR[:n-1]=q[::-1]
        L=np.fft.irfft(H*np.fft.rfft(bL,nfft),nfft)[:2*n-1]
        R=np.fft.irfft(H*np.fft.rfft(bR,nfft),nfft)[:2*n-1]
        const_left=np.empty(n); const_left[0]=0; const_left[1:]=np.cumsum(wp)
        const_right=np.empty(n); if n>1: const_right[:n-1]=np.cumsum(wn)[::-1]; const_right[n-1]=0
        grad_raw=const_left + const_right - L[:n] - R[n-1:2*n-1]
        grad_raw += w[n-1]*(1-2h)
        grad=grad_raw*dx
    diff = h.sum() - _OBJ_TARGET
    val += _OBJ_PEN*diff*diff
    grad += (2.0*_OBJ_PEN*diff)*np.ones(n)
    if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        val += 1e6
    return val, grad
```

`fix final exact sum`:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    s = h.sum()
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13: return h
    # choose index with value strictly inside
    interior = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0-1e-12))[0]
    if interior.size:
       idx=interior[0]
       old=h[idx]; new=np.clip(old+diff,0,1)
       h[idx]=new
    else:
       # distribute
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          if room.sum()>0: h += diff/room.sum()*room
       else:
          if h.sum()>0: h += diff/h.sum()*h
    # second pass maybe adjust another
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       # spread over all interior
       interior=np.where((h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12))[0]
       if interior.size: h[interior] += diff/interior.size
       else: h += diff/n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
Use in final. But if adjust one last element, sum may be exact within float. Good.

Let's design `alpha_schedule` exactly:
```
def alpha_schedule(h, n, limit, alphas, weights=None, pen=1e4):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(h,n)
    total = max(0.0, limit - time.time())
    if total <= 0 or len(alphas)==0: return best_h,best_c
    if weights is None: weights=[1.0]*len(alphas)
    wsum = float(sum(weights))
    t0_schedule = time.time()
    for i,a in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.time() >= limit - 2: break
        remaining = limit - time.time()
        # allocate proportional but allow leftover to later: fraction of original remaining? Use weight/wsum * (remaining + elapsed_alloc?) Maybe simpler:
        t_alloc = remaining * (weights[i] / (wsum - sum(weights[:i])))? That gives equal split of remaining over remaining weights.
        denom = wsum - sum(weights[:i])
        if denom <=0: break
        t_alloc = remaining * weights[i] / denom
        # cap maybe to not starve last? min 10 max remaining
        t_alloc = max(10.0, min(t_alloc, max(30.0, remaining*0.5)))
        phase_deadline = time.time() + t_alloc
        if i > 0:
            # choose pen higher for high alpha
            if a > 1e6: p = 1e5
            else: p = pen
        else: p=pen
        hc,cc = lbfgsb_with_limit(best_h, n, float(a), phase_deadline, pen=p)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c=cc; best_h=hc.copy()
    # final exact recompute
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h, best_c
```
But `denom = wsum - sum(weights[:i])` uses remaining sum of weights; if weights all positive. This allocates all remaining proportionally, good. `max(10, ...)` may exceed remaining if only one left; okay. If remaining small, no.

Let's implement a `pair_transfer_move` in local:
```
def try_top_lag_pairs(h, idxs, n, evalfn, best_c):
  h_work=h.copy(); best=...; improved=False
  for idx in idxs:
    lag=idx-(n-1); m=abs(lag)
    if m==0: continue
    if lag>0:
      diff = h[m:] - h[:n-m]
      # candidate positions: positive max, and top random among positive
      pos_idx = np.where(diff > 0.02)[0]
      if pos_idx.size:
         # choose argmax and one random
         choices = [pos_idx[np.argmax(diff[pos_idx])]]
         if pos_idx.size>1: choices.append(pos_idx[rng.randint(pos_idx.size)])
         for p in choices:
            i=p; j=p+m; t = min(diff[p]*0.5, 0.05, h[j]*0.8, (1-h[i])*0.8)
            cand=h_work.copy(); cand[i]+=t; cand[j]-=t; project; c=evalfn(cand); if improve accept h_work=cand; if global improve return
    else:
      diff = h[:n-m] - h[m:]
      choose diff>0, move from i to j: cand[i]-=t,cand[j]+=t
  return h_work,best,improved
```
But `evalfn` as argument top-level? We can pass `fft_eval` flag and call compute inside. Top-level function could take `fft_eval`. No lambda.

Let's implement `compute_all_lags_fft_scaled` with optional nfft. Use local? top-level.
```
def compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h,n):
    N=2*n-1
    nfft = 1 << (N-1).bit_length()
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    b_rev = 1.0 - h[::-1]? Wait c = h convolve (1-h_rev?) We need b = 1-h; b_rev = b[::-1]. `h[::-1]` no. Use b = 1.0 - h; b_rev = b[::-1]
    raw = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)*np.fft.rfft(b_rev,nfft), nfft)[:N]
    return raw * (2.0/n)
```
We cannot avoid b allocation. Good.

Pseudo local_search:
```
def local_search(h,n,deadline,rng,fft_eval=False,beta=50,max_dir=12,noise_scale=0.005):
    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    best_fast = compute_c5_fast(h,n,fft=fft_eval)
    best_exact = compute_c5(h,n)
    best_exact_h = h.copy()
    no_improve=0; it=0
    evalfn = use fast if fft_eval else exact for weights? We'll use `fast = fft_eval`
    while ...:
       if fft_eval: c_all = compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h,n)
       else: c_all = np.correlate(...) * dx
       Cfast = float(c_all.max())
       if Cfast < best_fast - 1e-15: best_fast=Cfast; best_h=h.copy()
       # exact periodic
       if fft_eval and (it % 25 == 0):
           cex=compute_c5(h,n); if cex < best_exact-1e-15: best_exact=cex; best_exact_h=h.copy()
       # build directions, accept with fast eval
       ... if accept:
           best_fast = ct; best_h=cand.copy(); h=cand
           if ct < best_fast? yes
           cex=compute_c5(cand,n) if (not fft_eval or it%25==0 or ct < old -1e-5?): ...
       it +=1
    # end: evaluate exact of best_h and best_exact_h
    c1=compute_c5(best_h,n); c2=compute_c5(best_exact_h,n)
    if c2 < c1: return best_exact_h,c2
    return best_h,c1
```
If `fft_eval=False`, fast=exact, no extra.

Helper functions:
```
def add_lag_gradient(grad, lag, h, n, weight):
    m = abs(lag)
    if m == 0:
        grad += weight * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    elif lag > 0:
        s = n - m
        grad[:s] += -weight * h[m:]
        grad[m:] += weight * (1.0 - h[:s])
    else:
        s = n - m
        grad[:s] += weight * (1.0 - h[m:])
        grad[m:] += -weight * h[:s]
```
Verified.

`transfer_direction`:
```
def transfer_direction(h, lag, n):
    d = np.zeros(n)
    m=abs(lag); s=n-m
    if m==0: return d
    if lag>0:
        diff = h[m:] - h[:s]
        d[:s] += diff
        d[m:] -= diff
    else:
        diff = h[:s] - h[m:]
        d[:s] -= diff
        d[m:] += diff
    return d
```
This can have entries sums. Mean subtract to preserve sum exactly? Each pair move equal opposite, sum zero in exact, but due diff arrays and overlapping indexes, sum of d may not be exactly zero due overlapping? It should: for each pair i,i+m, d_i += diff, d_{i+m} -= diff, sum contributions zero. Overlaps are fine linear; total sum zero. But floating maybe small. Do `d -= d.mean()`.

`binary_direction`:
```
def binary_direction(h):
    d = np.where(h >= 0.5, 1.0 - h, h)
    return d - d.mean()
```
This moves toward 1/0. Sum zero mean. Good.

Local search code snippet:
```
def local_search(...):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy()
  best_fast=compute_c5_fast(h,n,fft=fft_eval)
  best_exact=compute_c5(h,n)
  best_exact_h=h.copy()
  no_improve=0; it=0
  while time.time() < deadline:
    dx=2.0/n
    if fft_eval:
       c_all=compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h,n)
    else:
       c_all=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
    Cfast=float(np.max(c_all))
    if Cfast < best_fast - 1e-15:
       best_fast=Cfast; best_h=h.copy()
    if fft_eval and (it % 25 == 0):
       cex=compute_c5(h,n)
       if cex < best_exact - 1e-15:
          best_exact=cex; best_exact_h=h.copy()
    # weights
    z=beta*(c_all-Cfast)
    # z max 0, min negative; exp safe. However if Cfast from c_all max, z <=0. If c_all has NaN? no.
    ez=np.exp(z)
    w=ez/ez.sum()
    top=np.argsort(w)[::-1][:max_dir]
    improved=False
    # grad direction
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in top:
       wi=w[idx]
       if wi > 1e-10:
          add_lag_gradient(g, idx-(n-1), h, n, wi)
    g -= g.mean()
    gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
    if gmax > 1e-13:
       scale=0.04/(gmax+1e-15)
       dirv = -g
       for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
           cand = h + scale*f*dirv
           cand = project_feasible(cand,n)
           ct = compute_c5_fast(cand,n,fft=fft_eval)
           if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
               best_fast=ct; best_h=cand.copy(); h=cand; improved=True
               if fft_eval:
                   if (it % 25 == 0) or (ct < Cfast - 5e-5):
                       cex=compute_c5(cand,n)
                       if cex < best_exact - 1e-15:
                           best_exact=cex; best_exact_h=cand.copy()
               break
    # transfer directions for top up to 5
    if not improved:
       for idx in top[:5]:
          lag=int(idx-(n-1))
          if lag==0: continue
          d=transfer_direction(h,lag,n)
          d-=d.mean()
          dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
          if dmax <=1e-13: continue
          scale=0.04/(dmax+1e-15)
          for f in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
             cand=h+scale*f*d
             cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
             ct=compute_c5_fast(cand,n,fft=fft_eval)
             if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                ... break
          if improved: break
    # pair moves
    if not improved:
       for idx in top[:5]:
          lag=int(idx-(n-1)); m=abs(lag); s=n-m
          if m==0: continue
          if lag>0:
             diff = h[m:] - h[:s]
             mask = diff > 0.01
          else:
             diff = h[:s] - h[m:]
             mask = diff > 0.01
          if not np.any(mask): continue
          pos = np.where(mask)[0]
          # choose top 3 by diff and one random
          order = pos[np.argsort(-diff[pos])[:3]]
          if pos.size > 3:
             order = np.concatenate((order, [pos[rng.randint(pos.size)]]))
          for p in order:
             amp = diff[p]
             t = min(0.1, amp*0.5)
             cand = h.copy()
             if lag>0:
                i=p; j=p+m; cand[i]+=t; cand[j]-=t
             else:
                i=p; j=p+m; cand[i]-=t; cand[j]+=t
             cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
             ct=compute_c5_fast(cand,n,fft=fft_eval)
             if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                accept...
                if improved: break
          if improved: break
    # binary direction
    if not improved:
       d=binary_direction(h)
       dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
       if dmax>1e-13:
          scale=0.02/(dmax+1e-15)
          for f in [1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
             cand=h+scale*f*d
             cand=project_feasible(cand,n)
             ct=compute_c5_fast(cand,n,fft=fft_eval)
             if ct < best_fast - 1e-15: accept
    if improved:
       no_improve=0
    else:
       no_improve += 1
       if no_improve >= 15:
          noise = noise_scale * (1.0 + 0.05*min(50,no_improve))
          h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), n)
          no_improve = 0
          # update fast? not necessary
       if no_improve > 300: break
    it += 1
  # final compare exact
  c1=compute_c5(best_h,n)
  c2=compute_c5(best_exact_h,n)
  if c2 < c1:
      return best_exact_h, c2
  return best_h, c1
```
Need in accept update for `best_h` if fast improved, even if exact not. For `best_fast`, update. For exact, maybe if `not fft_eval`, exact same; but if `fft_eval=False`, `best_exact` should update too with ct:

Penalty gradient: `np.ones(n)` allocation each call. Could precompute? Not with variable n. Fine.

Now, performance of `alpha_schedule`: It calls `lbfgsb_with_limit` many times. `lbfgsb_with_limit` computes `best_c = compute_c5(h,n)` and uses L-BFGS. After each chunk, if no improvement 4 break. But if high alpha converges with no improvement quickly, `alpha_schedule` moves to next alpha. Good. But if low alpha no improvement due smoothing, break after 4 chunks; might waste less. Maybe 4 is too many; each chunk could be time large as estimated. But chunk loop uses maxit based on remaining, may run until phase deadline if improvements. If no improvement, no_improve counts per chunk; if each chunk is, say, 30s, 4=120s wasted if alpha bad. But low alphas likely improve or not? Could set no_improve>=2. Let's set 3. For high alpha, maybe first chunk no improvement because exact c not change but smooth improves? We only update if exact c improves; L-BFGSB may reduce smooth but exact max same? It should reduce exact eventually. But one chunk might not; 3 gives 3 chunks. Could be okay.

Set `max_chunk` perhaps 300 to avoid huge chunk no improve? Use callback. Let's pass max_chunk based on n: in `lbfgsb_with_limit`:

if n <=600: max_chunk=700

```
But function has n. Good.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_OBJ_N=0

class LBFTimeout(Exception): pass

def lbf_callback(xk):
    global _LBF_LAST_X
    _LBF_LAST_X = np.array(xk, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if time.time() >= _LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE:
        raise LBFTimeout()

`next_fast_len(n)`:
```
def next_fast_len(n):
    if n <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()
```
But `(n-1).bit_length()` method on int; if n Python int. Good.

`enforce_sum_exact` maybe use project? Could simply:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
  h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
  target = 0.5*n
  # Use project_feasible first for robust, then one-point adjust
  h = project_feasible(h,n)
  diff = target - h.sum()
  if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
     idxs = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
     if idxs.size:
        # spread among all interior? choose one with room
        i = idxs[np.argmin(np.maximum(h[idxs]-(0 if diff>0 else 0)?))]
        # Simpler: h[i] += diff clipped; if clipped, distribute to others
        new = h[i] + diff
        if 0 <= new <=1: h[i]=new
        else:
           # fallback project
           h=project_feasible(h,n)
     else:
       h=project_feasible(h,n)
  return h
```
Use project_feasible enough? It stops when abs(diff)<1e-15. So exact. `compute_c5` after project may not exactly sum but within. Fine. We can just use `project_feasible` final. But to be safe, use `project_feasible`.

Maybe a sum-exact one point can violate project? If sum error small, one interior exists. If not, project. Good.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(np.asarray(h,float), 1.0-np.asarray(h,float), mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
Avoid double asarray? h already.

Let's write local_search carefully:

```
def local_search(h, n, deadline, rng, fft_eval=False, beta=50.0, max_dir=12, noise_scale=0.005):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_fast = compute_c5_fast(h, n, fft=fft_eval)
    best_exact = compute_c5(h, n)
    best_exact_h = h.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if fft_eval:
            c_all = compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h, n)
        else:
            c_all = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)
        cmax = float(np.max(c_all))
        if cmax < best_fast - 1e-15:
            best_fast = cmax
            best_h = h.copy()
        z = beta * (c_all - cmax)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        w = ez / ez.sum()
        top = np.argsort(w)[::-1][:max_dir]
        improved = False
        # 1 gradient
        g = np.zeros(n)
        for idx in top:
            wi = w[idx]
            if wi > 1e-10:
                add_lag_gradient(g, int(idx - (n - 1)), h, n, float(wi))
        g -= g.mean()
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax > 1e-13:
            scale = 0.04 / (gmax + 1e-15)
            dirv = -g
            for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]:
                cand = h + scale * f * dirv
                cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                    best_fast = ct
                    best_h = cand.copy()
                    h = cand
                    improved = True
                    break
        # 2 transfer
        if not improved:
            for idx in top[:5]:
                lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                d = transfer_direction(h, lag, n)
                d -= d.mean()
                dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                if dmax <= 1e-13:
                    continue
                scale = 0.04 / (dmax + 1e-15)
                for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]:
                    cand = h + scale * f * d
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
        # 3 pair moves
        if not improved:
            for idx in top[:5]:
                lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
                m = abs(lag)
                s = n - m
                if m == 0:
                    continue
                if lag > 0:
                    diff = h[m:] - h[:s]
                    mask = diff > 0.01
                else:
                    diff = h[:s] - h[m:]
                    mask = diff > 0.01
                if not np.any(mask):
                    continue
                pos = np.where(mask)[0]
                top_pos = pos[np.argsort(-diff[pos])[:3]]
                if pos.size > 3:
                    top_pos = np.concatenate((top_pos, [pos[rng.randint(pos.size)]]))
                for p in top_pos:
                    amp = float(diff[p])
                    t = min(0.1, amp * 0.5)
                    if t <= 1e-9:
                        continue
                    cand = h.copy()
                    i = int(p)
                    j = i + m
                    if lag > 0:
                        cand[i] += t
                        cand[j] -= t
                    else:
                        cand[i] -= t
                        cand[j] += t
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
        # 4 binary direction
        if not improved:
            d = binary_direction(h)
            dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if dmax > 1e-13:
                scale = 0.02 / (dmax + 1e-15)
                for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05]:
                    cand = h + scale * f * d
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 15:
                noise = noise_scale * (1.0 + 0.05 * min(50.0, float(no_improve)))
                h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), n)
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve > 300:
                break
        if fft_eval and (it % 10 == 0):
            cex = compute_c5(h, n)
            if cex < best_exact - 1e-15:
                best_exact = cex
                best_exact_h = h.copy()
        it += 1
    c1 = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    c2 = compute_c5(best_exact_h, n)
    if c2 < c1:
        return best_exact_h, c2
    return best_h, c1
```
`best_fast` if fft_eval, but final exact. Good. Potential infinite if time loop and improve small? It counts. Good.
If `h` perturbed, cmax may be > best_fast, best_h remains; directions from perturbed h maybe not good until no_improve triggers. But random noise small.

`compute_all_lags_fft_scaled` returns c scaled; for `beta=50`, z range 0 to large negative. If c_all has max cmax, `ez` max 1. If cmax NaN? no.

`np.argsort(w)[::-1]` sorts by weight descending; but weights sum 1. Good. Could top by cmax? Weighted includes all near max. Fine.

In pair moves, for lag>0, mask diff>0.01 (later > earlier). Move t <=0.1 and amp*0.5. If h[j] lower than t? diff = h[j]-h[i] <=1-h[i], and h[j] >= diff? Since h[i]>=0, diff <=h[j]. t <=0.5*diff <=0.5*h[j], safe. For h[i]+t <=1? t<=0.5*(1-h[i])? diff <=1-h[i], t<=0.5 diff <=0.5(1-h[i]), safe. Good.
For lag<0, diff=earlier-later, move from earlier to later; safe. `project_feasible` ensures.

Now, `alpha_schedule`: Need ensure `weights` sum not zero. Use float.
```
def alpha_schedule(h, n, limit, alphas, weights=None, pen=1e4):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
   if len(alphas)==0 or time.time()>=limit: return best_h,best_c
   if weights is None: weights=[1.0]*len(alphas)
   weights=np.asarray(weights,dtype=float)
   wsum=weights.sum()
   remaining_sum=wsum
   for i,a in enumerate(alphas):
      if time.time() >= limit - 2: break
      remaining = limit - time.time()
      if remaining <= 5: break
      frac = weights[i]/remaining_sum
      t_alloc = remaining*frac
      t_alloc = max(8.0, min(t_alloc, max(30.0, remaining*0.6)))
      phase = time.time()+t_alloc
      p = pen
      if float(a) >= 1e6: p = max(pen, 1e5)
      hc,cc=lbfgsb_with_limit(best_h,n,float(a),phase,pen=p)
      if cc < best_c - 1e-15:
         best_c=cc; best_h=hc.copy()
      remaining_sum -= weights[i]
      if remaining_sum <=0: break
   best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   return best_h,best_c
```
If first weight fraction results t_alloc > remaining? frac sum remaining weights, t_alloc <= remaining. Then cap maybe. Good.
But `remaining_sum` after first maybe changes; if an earlier phase uses less than allocated, later can use more because remaining is updated and cumulative fractions based on remaining sum. Good.

`lbfgsb_with_limit` code with callback:
```
def lbfgsb_with_limit(h, n, alpha, deadline, pen=1e4, min_chunk=20, max_chunk=None):
    global _OBJ_N,_OBJ_NFFT,_OBJ_ALPHA,_OBJ_PEN,_OBJ_TARGET,_OBJ_DX,_LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE,_LBF_LAST_X
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if n <= 600: chunk_max=800
    elif n <= 1000: chunk_max=500
    else: chunk_max=300
    if max_chunk is None: max_chunk=chunk_max
    nfft=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    _OBJ_N=n; _OBJ_NFFT=nfft; _OBJ_ALPHA=float(alpha); _OBJ_PEN=float(pen); _OBJ_TARGET=0.5*n; _OBJ_DX=2.0/n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 2: break
       t0=time.time()
       try:
          val,grad=lbf_objective_grad(h)
       except Exception:
          break
       t_eval=max(1e-6, time.time()-t0)
       maxit=int(rem / (30.0*t_eval))
       if maxit < min_chunk: maxit=min_chunk
       if maxit > max_chunk: maxit=max_chunk
       if maxit <=0: break
       _LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE = deadline - 1e-6
       _LBF_LAST_X = None
       t0=time.time()
       try:
          res = minimize(lbf_objective_grad, h, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                         options={'maxiter': maxit, 'gtol': 1e-11, 'ftol': 1e-18, 'maxls': 25, 'iprint': -1},
                         callback=lbf_callback)
          h_new = res.x
       except LBFTimeout:
          h_new = _LBF_LAST_X
          _LBF_LAST_X = None
          if h_new is None:
             break
       except Exception:
          break
       _LBF_LAST_X = None
       h_new = np.clip(np.asarray(h_new, dtype=float).reshape(n),0,1)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)):
          break
       h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
       c = compute_c5(h_new, n)
       if c < best_c - 1e-15:
           best_c = c; best_h = h_new.copy(); h = h_new; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           # maybe keep h if line search improved smooth? If exact not, maybe h_new still better for successive? Use h_new anyway? If exact worse but smooth maybe lower, continuing from h_new could diverge. We should set h = h_new if finite even if exact not improved? Could be moving to flat exact but lower smooth; helpful. But if exact worse due projection, maybe not. Keep h = h_new? L-BFGS next from h_new. I think yes, because smooth objective improved; exact may stagnate. But if exact worsened, continuing may recover. However best_h tracks best exact. Let's set h = h_new always (if finite) to follow smooth.
       h = h_new  # put after? But if improved already set.
       if no_improve >= 3:
           # maybe if res.status==0 break
           if res.status in (0,2,3,6) maybe break? If status 0 break; otherwise maybe one more. Simpler break if no_improve>=3.
           break
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
    return best_h, float(best_c)
```

Now, `threshold_binary`:
```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
    hb = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    target = int(round(0.5*n))
    ones = int(hb.sum())
    if ones > target:
       idx = np.where(hb == 1)[0]
       order = np.argsort(h[idx])
       for t in order[:ones-target]: hb[idx[t]]=0
    elif ones < target:
       idx = np.where(hb == 0)[0]
       order = np.argsort(-h[idx])
       for t in order[:target-ones]: hb[idx[t]]=1
    return project_feasible(hb,n)
```
If n odd, target int floor? `round(250.5)=250` (bankers? Python round 250.5 ->250). But target should be 250.5; binary cannot. threshold_count 250 leaves sum deficit 0.5; project adds fractional. OK. But if `ones < target` and idx size maybe less? If h<0.5 for none? If all ones and n odd? target 250, ones500 remove 250 -> okay. If all zeros, target250, add. Good.

    if budget_s > 400:

              # maybe still if talloc large? skip
              continue
          alphas,weights = high_alphas(q)
          hc,cc=alpha_schedule(h_start,q,limit_lb,alphas,weights,pen=1e4)
          # local high
          limit_ls=min(deadline, time.time()+0.3*talloc)
          fft = q > 800
          hc,cc=local_search(hc,q,limit_ls,rng,fft_eval=fft,beta=60,max_dir=12,noise_scale=0.004)
          # exact already local returns exact
          if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
              best_c=cc; best_h=hc; best_n=q
              # optional quick local exact if not fft
              if fft and time.time() < deadline-20:
                  hc2,cc2=local_search(best_h,best_n,min(deadline,time.time()+30),rng,fft_eval=False,beta=100,max_dir=8,noise_scale=0.001)
                  if cc2<best_c: update
```

    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       fft = best_n > 1200

       hc,cc=local_search(best_h,best_n, time.time()+alloc, rng, fft_eval=fft, beta=100, max_dir=10, noise_scale=0.001)

def alphas_for_n(n):
  if n <= 600:
    return [100.0,500.0,2000.0,10000.0,50000.0,200000.0,1000000.0,5000000.0,20000000.0,100000000.0]
  elif n <= 1000:
    return [500.0,2000.0,10000.0,50000.0,200000.0,1000000.0,5000000.0,20000000.0,100000000.0]
  else:
    return [2000.0,10000.0,50000.0,200000.0,1000000.0,5000000.0,20000000.0,100000000.0]

def default_weights(alphas):

`upsample_constant`: if n_dst <= n_src? If same return project; if smaller, use downsample maybe. We only call increasing. But if best_n became q and next q same? skip. Could define:
```
def resample_constant(h_src,n_src,n_dst):
  if n_dst == n_src: return project_feasible(h_src,n_dst)
  if n_dst > n_src:
     idx = np.floor(((np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(n_src/n_dst))).astype(np.int64)
     idx=np.clip(idx,0,n_src-1)
     h_dst=h_src[idx]
  else:
     h_dst=zeros(n_dst); counts=zeros(n_dst)
     old_to_new=np.floor(((np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(n_dst/n_src))).astype(int)
     old_to_new=clip(0,n_dst-1)
     np.add.at(h_dst, old_to_new, h_src)
     counts=np.bincount(old_to_new, minlength=n_dst).astype(float)
     h_dst[counts>0] /= counts[counts>0]
  return project_feasible(h_dst,n_dst)
```
Use for high. Good.

Now, check if `np.floor(((np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(n_src/n_dst)))` with n_dst > n_src yields idx 0 for many, n_src-1 for last? Let's write run with conditions:

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    # initial
    try:
       h_seq = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       h_seq = None
    if h_seq is None or h_seq.size == 0:
       h_seq = default_h(500, rng)
    h_seq = h_seq.copy()
    base_n = int(h_seq.size)
    h0 = project_feasible(h_seq, base_n)
    best_h = h0.copy(); best_n=base_n; best_c=compute_c5(h0,best_n)
    B = float(budget_s)
    # Parent LB
    if B > 180:
       alloc = min(300.0, max(60.0, 0.25*B))
       limit = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
       alphas = alphas_for_n(best_n)
       hc,cc = alpha_schedule(best_h,best_n,limit,alphas,default_weights(alphas),pen=1e4)
       if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
          best_c=cc; best_h=hc
    # Parent local
    if time.time() < deadline - 20 and B > 100:
       alloc = min(100.0, max(30.0, 0.08*B))
       limit = min(deadline, time.time()+alloc)
       hc,cc = local_search(best_h,best_n,limit,rng,fft_eval=False,beta=60.0,max_dir=12,noise_scale=0.004)
       if cc < best_c - 1e-14: update
    # Binary restart
    if time.time() < deadline - 30 and B > 300:
       alloc = min(70.0, max(25.0, 0.05*B))
       hb = threshold_binary(best_h,best_n)
       cb = compute_c5(hb,best_n)
       if cb <= best_c + 0.12:
          limit1 = min(deadline, time.time()+0.6*alloc)
          alphasb = [1000.0,10000.0,100000.0,1000000.0,10000000.0]
          hc,cc=alpha_schedule(hb,best_n,limit1,alphasb,default_weights(alphasb),pen=1e5)
          if cc < best_c - 1e-14: update
          if time.time() < deadline-10:
             limit2=min(deadline,time.time()+0.4*alloc)
             hc2,cc2=local_search(hc,best_n,limit2,rng,fft_eval=False,beta=60,max_dir=10,noise_scale=0.003)
             if cc2 < best_c -1e-14: update
    # high resolutions
    if B > 500:
       candidates=[]
       for factor in [1.4,2.0,3.0,4.0]:
          q=int(round(base_n*factor))
          if q > best_n and q <= 2500 and q not in candidates:
             candidates.append(q)
       fracs=[0.28,0.34,0.24,0.14]
       for idx,q in enumerate(candidates):
          if time.time() > deadline - 70: break
          if q <= best_n: continue
          rem=deadline-time.time()
          if rem < 90: break
          frac=fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.12
          talloc=max(45.0, rem*frac)
          if talloc > rem: talloc=rem
          t_lb=0.72*talloc
          t_ls=0.28*talloc
          h_start=resample_constant(best_h,best_n,q)
          c_start=compute_c5(h_start,q)
          if c_start > best_c + 0.06:
             continue
          limit1=min(deadline, time.time()+t_lb)
          alphas=alphas_for_n(q)
          # For high, maybe reduce alphas by using subset if t_lb short
          if t_lb < 80:
             alphas=alphas[3:]  # higher alphas
          hc,cc=alpha_schedule(h_start,q,limit1,alphas,default_weights(alphas),pen=1e4)
          limit2=min(deadline, time.time()+t_ls)
          hh,cc=local_search(hc,q,limit2,rng,fft_eval=(q>800),beta=60,max_dir=12,noise_scale=0.004)
          if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=cc; best_h=hh; best_n=q
             if q>800 and time.time() < deadline-20:
                hh2,cc2=local_search(best_h,best_n,min(deadline,time.time()+30.0),rng,fft_eval=False,beta=100,max_dir=8,noise_scale=0.001)
                if cc2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                   best_c=cc2; best_h=hh2
    # Final exact local
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       fft = best_n > 1200
       alloc = min(60.0, max(15.0, deadline-time.time()-2.0))
       hc,cc=local_search(best_h,best_n,time.time()+alloc,rng,fft_eval=fft,beta=100.0,max_dir=10,noise_scale=0.001)
       if cc < best_c - 1e-14: update
    # Final L-BFGS
    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
       hc,cc=lbfgsb_with_limit(best_h,best_n,1e8,min(deadline,time.time()+25.0),pen=1e6,max_chunk=200)
       if cc < best_c - 1e-14: update
    best_h=enforce_sum_exact(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

def simple_variants_best(h,n):
    best_h=np.array(h,float); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    variants = [best_h.copy(), best_h[::-1], 1.0-best_h[::-1], 1.0-best_h]

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


_OBJ_N = 0
_OBJ_NFFT = 0
_OBJ_ALPHA = 1.0
_OBJ_PEN = 0.0
_OBJ_TARGET = 0.0
_OBJ_DX = 0.0
_LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE = 0.0
_LBF_LAST_X = None


class LBFTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def lbf_callback(xk):
    global _LBF_LAST_X
    _LBF_LAST_X = np.array(xk, dtype=float, copy=True)
    if time.time() >= _LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE:
        raise LBFTimeout()


def next_fast_len(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(n) - 1).bit_length()


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * int(n)

    for _ in range(1000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    s = float(h.sum())
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        interior = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if interior.size:
            i = interior[0]
            new_val = h[i] + diff
            if 0.0 <= new_val <= 1.0:
                h[i] = new_val
        else:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)

    return h


def enforce_sum_exact(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    target = 0.5 * int(n)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
        return h

    interior = np.where((h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
    if interior.size:
        i = interior[np.argmin(np.maximum(h[interior], 1.0 - h[interior]))]
        new_val = h[i] + diff
        if 0.0 <= new_val <= 1.0:
            h[i] = new_val
            return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return project_feasible(h, n)


def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    N = 2 * int(n) - 1
    nfft = next_fast_len(N)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1]
    raw = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(h, nfft) * np.fft.rfft(b_rev, nfft), nfft)[:N]
    return raw * (2.0 / float(n))


def compute_all_lags_scaled(h, n, fft=False):
    if fft:
        return compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h, n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5_fast(h, n, fft=False):
    if fft:
        c = compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h, n)
        return float(np.max(c))
    return compute_c5(h, n)


def smooth_max_value_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = float(alpha) * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z - mx)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        nm = (c == m)
        if not np.any(nm):
            nm[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        w[nm] = 1.0
        w /= w.sum()
        return m, w
    w = ez / s
    val = m + (mx + np.log(s)) / float(alpha)
    return val, w


def add_lag_gradient(grad, lag, h, n, weight):
    m = abs(int(lag))
    if m == 0:
        grad += float(weight) * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        return
    s = int(n) - m
    if int(lag) > 0:
        grad[:s] += -float(weight) * h[m:]
        grad[m:] += float(weight) * (1.0 - h[:s])
    else:
        grad[:s] += float(weight) * (1.0 - h[m:])
        grad[m:] += -float(weight) * h[:s]


def transfer_direction(h, lag, n):
    d = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=float)
    m = abs(int(lag))
    if m == 0:
        return d
    s = int(n) - m
    if int(lag) > 0:
        diff = h[m:] - h[:s]
        d[:s] += diff
        d[m:] -= diff
    else:
        diff = h[:s] - h[m:]
        d[:s] -= diff
        d[m:] += diff
    return d


def binary_direction(h):
    d = np.where(h >= 0.5, 1.0 - h, h)
    return d - d.mean()


def lbf_objective_grad(x):
    n = int(_OBJ_N)
    nfft = int(_OBJ_NFFT)
    dx = float(_OBJ_DX)
    alpha = float(_OBJ_ALPHA)
    pen = float(_OBJ_PEN)
    target = float(_OBJ_TARGET)

    h = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(n), 0.0, 1.0)

    H = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
    b = 1.0 - h
    b_rev = b[::-1]
    B = np.fft.rfft(b_rev, nfft)
    c_raw = np.fft.irfft(H * B, nfft)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = c_raw * dx

    val, w = smooth_max_value_weights(c, alpha)

    active = np.where(w > 1e-12)[0]
    if active.size < max(64, n // 8):
        grad_raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for idx in active:
            idx = int(idx)
            add_lag_gradient(grad_raw, idx - (n - 1), h, n, float(w[idx]))
        grad = grad_raw * dx
    else:
        wp = w[n:n]
        wn = w[n - 2::-1]
        q = wp + wn

        bL = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        bL[1:] = q
        L = np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(bL, nfft), nfft)[:2 * n - 1][:n]

        bR = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        bR[:n - 1] = q[::-1]
        R = np.fft.irfft(H * np.fft.rfft(bR, nfft), nfft)[:2 * n - 1][n - 1:2 * n - 1]

        const_left = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        if n > 1:
            const_left[1:] = np.cumsum(wp)

        const_right = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        if n > 1:
            const_right[:n - 1] = np.cumsum(wn)[::-1]

        grad_raw = const_left + const_right - L - R
        grad_raw += float(w[n - 1]) * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        grad = grad_raw * dx

    diff = float(h.sum()) - target
    val += pen * diff * diff
    grad += (2.0 * pen * diff) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val += 1e12
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    return float(val), grad


def lbfgsb_with_limit(h, n, alpha, deadline, pen=1e4, min_chunk=20, max_chunk=None):
    global _OBJ_N, _OBJ_NFFT, _OBJ_ALPHA, _OBJ_PEN, _OBJ_TARGET, _OBJ_DX
    global _LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE, _LBF_LAST_X

    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    h_c = best_c

    if max_chunk is None:
        if n <= 600:
            max_chunk = 800
        elif n <= 1000:
            max_chunk = 500
        else:
            max_chunk = 300

    nfft = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    _OBJ_N = n
    _OBJ_NFFT = nfft
    _OBJ_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    _OBJ_PEN = float(pen)
    _OBJ_TARGET = 0.5 * n
    _OBJ_DX = 2.0 / float(n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 2.0:
            break

        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            _lbf_objective_grad(h)
        except Exception:
            break
        t_eval = max(1e-6, time.time() - t0)

        maxit = int(rem / (30.0 * t_eval))
        if maxit < min_chunk:
            maxit = min_chunk
        if maxit > max_chunk:
            maxit = max_chunk
        if maxit <= 0:
            break

        _LBF_CALLBACK_DEADLINE = deadline - 1e-6
        _LBF_LAST_X = None

        timeout_abort = False
        h_new = None
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbf_objective_grad,
                h,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                callback=lbf_callback,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "ftol": 1e-18,
                    "maxls": 25,
                    "maxfun": max(1000, maxit * 40),
                    "iprint": -1,
                },
            )
            h_new = res.x
        except LBFTimeout:
            timeout_abort = True
            h_new = _LBF_LAST_X
            _LBF_LAST_X = None
            if h_new is None:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

        _LBF_LAST_X = None
        h_new = np.clip(np.asarray(h_new, dtype=float).reshape(n), 0.0, 1.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h_new)):
            break

        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        new_c = compute_c5(h_new, n)

        if new_c < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = new_c
            best_h = h_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if new_c > h_c + 1e-12:
            h = best_h.copy()
            h_c = best_c
        else:
            h = h_new
            h_c = new_c

        if timeout_abort:
            break
        if no_improve >= 3:
            break

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def default_weights(alphas):
    return [1.0 + 0.25 * float(i) for i in range(len(alphas))]


def alpha_schedule(h, n, limit, alphas, weights=None, pen=1e4):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    if len(alphas) == 0 or time.time() >= limit:
        return best_h, best_c

    if weights is None:
        weights = default_weights(alphas)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)
    if weights.size != len(alphas) or np.any(weights <= 0):
        weights = default_weights(alphas)

    remaining_sum = float(weights.sum())
    for i, a in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.time() >= limit - 2.0:
            break
        remaining = limit - time.time()
        if remaining <= 6.0:
            break
        if remaining_sum <= 0:
            break
        frac = float(weights[i]) / remaining_sum
        t_alloc = remaining * frac
        t_alloc = max(8.0, min(t_alloc, max(30.0, 0.6 * remaining)))
        phase_deadline = time.time() + t_alloc

        p = float(pen)
        if float(a) >= 1e6:
            p = max(p, 1e5)

        hc, cc = lbfgsb_with_limit(best_h, n, float(a), phase_deadline, pen=p)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc.copy()

        remaining_sum -= float(weights[i])

    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def alphas_for_n(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 600:
        return [
            100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0,
            200000.0, 1000000.0, 5000000.0, 20000000.0, 100000000.0,
        ]
    if n <= 1000:
        return [
            500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0,
            1000000.0, 5000000.0, 20000000.0, 100000000.0,
        ]
    return [
        2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0,
        1000000.0, 5000000.0, 20000000.0, 100000000.0,
    ]


def local_search(h, n, deadline, rng, fft_eval=False, beta=50.0, max_dir=12, noise_scale=0.005):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_fast = compute_c5_fast(h, n, fft=fft_eval)
    best_exact = compute_c5(h, n)
    best_exact_h = h.copy()

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if fft_eval:
            c_all = compute_all_lags_fft_scaled(h, n)
        else:
            c_all = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))

        cmax = float(np.max(c_all))
        if cmax < best_fast - 1e-15:
            best_fast = cmax
            best_h = h.copy()

        z = float(beta) * (c_all - cmax)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        w = ez / ez.sum()
        top = np.argsort(w)[::-1][:max_dir]

        improved = False

        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for idx in top:
            idx = int(idx)
            wi = float(w[idx])
            if wi > 1e-10:
                add_lag_gradient(g, idx - (n - 1), h, n, wi)
        g -= g.mean()
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax > 1e-13:
            scale = 0.04 / (gmax + 1e-15)
            dirv = -g
            for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005]:
                cand = h + scale * f * dirv
                cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                    best_fast = ct
                    best_h = cand.copy()
                    h = cand
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            for idx in top[:5]:
                lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
                if lag == 0:
                    continue
                d = transfer_direction(h, lag, n)
                d -= d.mean()
                dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
                if dmax <= 1e-13:
                    continue
                scale = 0.04 / (dmax + 1e-15)
                for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]:
                    cand = h + scale * f * d
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            for idx in top[:5]:
                lag = int(idx - (n - 1))
                m = abs(lag)
                s = n - m
                if m == 0:
                    continue
                if lag > 0:
                    diff = h[m:] - h[:s]
                    mask = diff > 0.01
                else:
                    diff = h[:s] - h[m:]
                    mask = diff > 0.01
                if not np.any(mask):
                    continue

                pos = np.where(mask)[0]
                top_pos = pos[np.argsort(-diff[pos])[:3]]
                if pos.size > 3:
                    top_pos = np.concatenate((top_pos, [pos[int(rng.randint(pos.size))]]))

                for p in top_pos:
                    amp = float(diff[p])
                    t = min(0.1, 0.5 * amp)
                    if t <= 1e-9:
                        continue
                    cand = h.copy()
                    i = int(p)
                    j = i + m
                    if lag > 0:
                        cand[i] += t
                        cand[j] -= t
                    else:
                        cand[i] -= t
                        cand[j] += t
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            d = binary_direction(h)
            dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if dmax > 1e-13:
                scale = 0.02 / (dmax + 1e-15)
                for f in [1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05]:
                    cand = h + scale * f * d
                    cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
                    ct = compute_c5_fast(cand, n, fft=fft_eval)
                    if ct < best_fast - 1e-15:
                        best_fast = ct
                        best_h = cand.copy()
                        h = cand
                        improved = True
                        break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 15:
                noise = noise_scale * (1.0 + 0.05 * min(50.0, float(no_improve)))
                h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n), n)
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve > 300:
                break

        if fft_eval and (it % 10 == 0):
            cex = compute_c5(h, n)
            if cex < best_exact - 1e-15:
                best_exact = cex
                best_exact_h = h.copy()

        it += 1

    c1 = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    c2 = compute_c5(best_exact_h, n)
    if c2 < c1:
        return best_exact_h, c2
    return best_h, c1


def threshold_binary(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    hb = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))

    ones = int(hb.sum())
    if ones > target:
        idx = np.where(hb == 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            order = np.argsort(h[idx])
            for t in order[:max(0, ones - target)]:
                hb[idx[int(t)]] = 0.0
    elif ones < target:
        idx = np.where(hb == 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            order = np.argsort(-h[idx])
            for t in order[:max(0, target - ones)]:
                hb[idx[int(t)]] = 1.0

    return project_feasible(hb, n)


def resample_constant(h_src, n_src, n_dst):
    n_src = int(n_src)
    n_dst = int(n_dst)
    h_src = np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float)

    if n_dst == n_src:
        return project_feasible(h_src, n_dst)

    if n_dst > n_src:
        idx = np.floor(((np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (n_src / float(n_dst)))).astype(np.int64)
        idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n_src - 1)
        h_dst = h_src[idx]
    else:
        old_to_new = np.floor(((np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (n_dst / float(n_src)))).astype(np.int64)
        old_to_new = np.clip(old_to_new, 0, n_dst - 1)
        h_dst = np.zeros(n_dst, dtype=float)
        np.add.at(h_dst, old_to_new, h_src)
        counts = np.bincount(old_to_new, minlength=n_dst).astype(float)
        nz = counts > 0
        h_dst[nz] /= counts[nz]

    return project_feasible(h_dst, n_dst)


def simple_variants_best(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    variants = [
        best_h.copy(),
        best_h[::-1],
        1.0 - best_h[::-1],
        1.0 - best_h,
        0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]),
        0.5 * (best_h + (1.0 - best_h[::-1])),
    ]

    r = min(5, max(1, n // 2))
    for s in range(1, r + 1):
        variants.append(np.roll(best_h, s))
        variants.append(np.roll(best_h, -s))

    for v in variants:
        vv = project_feasible(v, n)
        c = compute_c5(vv, n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = c
            best_h = vv

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def default_h(n, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[:int(n // 2)] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    B = float(budget_s)
    t0 = time.time()
    margin = min(10.0, max(0.5, 0.02 * B))
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, B - margin)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        h_seq = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h_seq = None

    if h_seq is None or int(h_seq.size) == 0:
        h_seq = default_h(500, rng)

    base_n = int(h_seq.size)
    h0 = project_feasible(h_seq, base_n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = base_n
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    hc, cc = simple_variants_best(best_h, best_n)
    if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
        best_c = cc
        best_h = hc

    if B > 180.0:
        alloc = min(300.0, max(60.0, 0.25 * B))
        limit = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        alphas = alphas_for_n(best_n)
        hc, cc = alpha_schedule(best_h, best_n, limit, alphas, default_weights(alphas), pen=1e4)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc

    if time.time() < deadline - 20.0 and B > 100.0:
        alloc = min(100.0, max(30.0, 0.08 * B))
        limit = min(deadline, time.time() + alloc)
        hc, cc = local_search(
            best_h, best_n, limit, rng,
            fft_eval=False, beta=60.0, max_dir=12, noise_scale=0.004
        )
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc

    hc, cc = simple_variants_best(best_h, best_n)
    if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
        best_c = cc
        best_h = hc

    if time.time() < deadline - 30.0 and B > 300.0:
        alloc = min(70.0, max(25.0, 0.05 * B))
        hb = threshold_binary(best_h, best_n)
        cb = compute_c5(hb, best_n)
        if cb <= best_c + 0.12:
            limit1 = min(deadline, time.time() + 0.6 * alloc)
            alphasb = [1000.0, 10000.0, 100000.0, 1000000.0, 10000000.0]
            hc, cc = alpha_schedule(hb, best_n, limit1, alphasb, default_weights(alphasb), pen=1e5)
            if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hc

            if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
                limit2 = min(deadline, time.time() + 0.4 * alloc)
                hc2, cc2 = local_search(
                    hc, best_n, limit2, rng,
                    fft_eval=False, beta=60.0, max_dir=10, noise_scale=0.003
                )
                if cc2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = cc2
                    best_h = hc2

    if B > 500.0:
        candidates = []
        for factor in [1.4, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0]:
            q = int(round(base_n * float(factor)))
            if q > best_n and q <= 2500 and q not in candidates:
                candidates.append(q)

        fracs = [0.28, 0.34, 0.24, 0.14]
        for idx, q in enumerate(candidates):
            if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            if q <= best_n:
                continue
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 90.0:
                break

            frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.12
            talloc = max(45.0, rem * frac)
            if talloc > rem:
                talloc = rem

            t_lb = 0.72 * talloc
            t_ls = 0.28 * talloc

            h_start = resample_constant(best_h, best_n, q)
            c_start = compute_c5(h_start, q)
            if c_start > best_c + 0.06:
                continue

            limit1 = min(deadline, time.time() + t_lb)
            alphas = alphas_for_n(q)
            if t_lb < 80.0 and len(alphas) > 3:
                alphas = alphas[3:]
            hc, cc = alpha_schedule(h_start, q, limit1, alphas, default_weights(alphas), pen=1e4)

            limit2 = min(deadline, time.time() + t_ls)
            hh, cc = local_search(
                hc, q, limit2, rng,
                fft_eval=(q > 800), beta=60.0, max_dir=12, noise_scale=0.004
            )

            if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hh
                best_n = q

                hh, cc = simple_variants_best(best_h, best_n)
                if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = hh

                if q > 800 and time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
                    hh2, cc2 = local_search(
                        best_h, best_n, min(deadline, time.time() + 30.0), rng,
                        fft_eval=False, beta=100.0, max_dir=8, noise_scale=0.001
                    )
                    if cc2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = cc2
                        best_h = hh2

    if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
        fft = best_n > 1200
        alloc = min(60.0, max(15.0, deadline - time.time() - 2.0))
        hc, cc = local_search(
            best_h, best_n, time.time() + alloc, rng,
            fft_eval=fft, beta=100.0, max_dir=10, noise_scale=0.001
        )
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc

    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        hc, cc = lbfgsb_with_limit(
            best_h, best_n, 1e8,
            min(deadline, time.time() + 25.0),
            pen=1e6, max_chunk=200
        )
        if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc

    best_h = enforce_sum_exact(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
